# SQLite Typed Transport Prerequisite

The actual SqliteSnapshotControl, NativeEncodingBound, Projection and Reconstruction owners in general Framework IO return String after losing SqliteSnapshotError cancellation and limit identity. Actual OS Store record emit/read callbacks erase ValueError, TextError, PackError and controlled SemioError into that intermediate String chain. Higher procedural, Office, Note, Playbook and Raster consumers then reconstruct TextError without original authority. This chain must retain owned causes before the higher constructor floor can honestly close.

Canonical generic owners are Framework IO/🪶️sqlite-snapshot/🦀️.rs and Framework IO/🪶️sqlite-snapshot/🧩️artifact/🦀️.rs; generic IO root also consumes the controls. Actual OS Store callbacks live 📦️codec/🪶️snapshot-capability/🛫️native-encoding and 🛬️native-decoding; ArtifactSqliteSnapshot declarations live in actual OS Store root. No transport production was edited by this capture.

491 current lexical source candidates (12051569 bytes) and 847 parsed function bodies are retained in generated/value-refusal/sqlite-transport-current-full-before-1.json, including complete sources, inverses, bytes and SHA256. This is a finite candidate roster, not a provider-closed native epoch.
